=== function getRegularTickerMessages @ 1622930 ===
map(function(x) {
        return `🕌 ${x.name} prayer during your shift is at ${x.value}.`;
      });
    }

    // Once every prayer within the shift has passed, do not announce an
    // unrelated prayer outside that doctor's working period.
    if (now >= shift.startMinutes && now <= shift.endMinutes) {
      return [];
    }
  }

  // Outside a scheduled shift, keep the general "next prayer" behaviour.
  const upcoming = prayers.filter(function(x) {
    return x.minute >= now;
  });

  if (upcoming.length) {
    return [`🕌 ${upcoming[0].name} prayer today is at ${upcoming[0].value}.`];
  }

  // After Isha, retain the earlier behaviour of pointing to tomorrow's Fajr.
  if (prayerTimes.Fajr) {
    return [`🕌 The next prayer is Fajr tomorrow at ${prayerTimes.Fajr}.`];
  }

  return [];
}

function normalizeFeedTitle(value) {
  return String(value || '')
    .replace(/<[^>]*>/g, '')
    .replace(/&amp;/g, '&')
    .replace(/&#39;/g, "'")
    .replace(/&quot;/g, '"')
    .trim();
}

async function fetchRssJson(url) {
  const response = await fetch(url, {headers:{'Accept':'application/json'}});
  if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Feed request failed');
  const payload = await response.json();
  return Array.isArray(payload.items) ? payload.items : [];
}

function checkAndRenderBreakingNews() {
  // Unified priority stream:
  // During the first hour of an affected shift:
  // sick leave every 2 messages > prayer every 5 messages > admin notices > clinical/general.
  // After that first hour the sick alert loses ticker priority and normal rotation resumes.
  // Normal content follows the exact 11-step cycle defined in renderNextTickerSlot().
  if (headlineTickerTimer) {
    clearTimeout(headlineTickerTimer);
    headlineTickerTimer = null;
  }
  renderNextTickerSlot();
}

function getApprovedRotaMonthLabel() {
  try {
    const first = (currentSchedule || [])[0];
    const raw = first && (first.date || first.isoDate || first.dayDate);
    if (raw) {
      return new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-GB', {timeZone:'Asia/Qatar', month:'long', year:'numeric'}).format(new Date(raw));
    }
  } catch(e) {}
  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-GB', {timeZone:'Asia/Qatar', month:'long', year:'numeric'}).format(new Date());
}

function showMoursiApprovalExportPopup() {
  let modal = document.getElementById('moursiApprovalExportModal');
  if (!modal) {
    modal = document.createElement('div');
    modal.id = 'moursiApprovalExportModal';
    modal.className = 'fixed inset-0 z-[10050] hidden items-center justify-center bg-slate-950/80 backdrop-blur-sm p-4';
    modal.innerHTML = `
      <div class="w-full max-w-sm rounded-2xl border border-emerald-500/30 bg-slate-900 p-5 shadow-2xl">
        <div class="flex items-start gap-3">
          <div class="grid h-10 w-10 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-xl bg-emerald-500/15 text-emerald-300"><i class="fa-solid fa-check-double"></i></div>
          <div>
            <h3 class="text-sm font-black text-white">Rota approved</h3>
            <p class="mt-1 text-[11px] leading-relaxed text-slate-400">The rota is now released to colleagues and they have been notified. Would you like to export the approved rota?</p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="mt-5 grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
          <button class="rounded-xl bg-rose-600 px-3 py-3 text-xs font-black text-white hover:bg-rose-500" onclick="completeMoursiApprovalExport('pdf')"><i class="fa-solid fa-file-pdf mr-1.5"></i>PDF</button>
          <button class="rounded-xl bg-cyan-600 px-3 py-3 text-xs font-black text-white hover:bg-cyan-500" onclick="completeMoursiApprovalExport('png')"><i class="fa-regular fa-image mr-1.5"></i>PNG</button>
        </div>
        <button class="mt-2 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-800 px-3 py-2.5 text-xs font-bold text-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-700" onclick="completeMoursiApprovalExport('none')">Not now</button>
      </div>`;
    document.body.appendChild(modal);
  }
  modal.classList.remove('hidden');
  modal.classList.add('flex');
}

function completeMoursiApprovalExport(kind) {
  const modal = document.getElementById('moursiApprovalExportModal');
  if (modal) { modal.classList.add('hidden'); modal.classList.remove('flex'); }
  try {
    if (kind === 'pdf') exportToPDFReport();
    if (kind === 'png') exportToImageReport();
  } catch(e) { console.warn('Approved rota export:', e); }
  setTimeout(function() {
    const assign = confirm('Do you want to assign shift leads?');
    if (assign) {
      Storage.set('rotastic_shift_lead_assignment_after_approval', 'true');
      switchTab('generator');
      setTimeout(function() {
        const target = document.getElementById('assignShiftLeadAM');
        if (target) { target.scrollIntoView({behavior:'smooth', block:'center'}); target.focus(); }
      }, 180);
    } else {
      Storage.set('rotastic_shift_lead_assignment_after_approval', 'false');
    }
  }, 250);
}

async function notifyEveryoneRotaApproved(workflow) {
  const uniqueDoctors = Array.from(new Set((DOCTORS || []).map(function(d){ return d.name; }).filter(Boolean)));
  const message = 'The physician rota for ' + getApprovedRotaMonthLabel() + ' has been approved by the Clinical Lead and is now available in your Rota view.';
  uniqueDoctors.forEach(function(name) {
    queueInAppNotification(name, message, 'rota-approved', {workflowId:workflow.id, action:'rota-approved'});
  });
  requestBrowserNotificationPermission();
  if (ROTASTIC_PUSH_BROADCAST_ENDPOINT) {
    try {
      await rotasticPushBackendFetch({
        method:'POST',
        headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},
        body:JSON.stringify({
          type:'rota-approved', audience:'all-colleagues',
          title:'ROTASTIC — Rota Approved ✅', body:message,
          workflowId:workflow.id, createdAt:new Date().toISOString()
        })
      });
    } catch(e) { console.warn('Approved rota broadcast push failed:', e); }
  }
}

function approveRotaAsClinicalLead() {
  if (activeUserDocName !== 'Dr Mohamed M.') {
    alert('Only the Clinical Lead can approve a rota sent for review.');
    return;
  }

  const workflow = getRotaApprovalWorkflow();
  if (!workflow || !workflow.sentAt) {
    alert('There is no rota currently awaiting approval.');
    return;
  }

  workflow.status = 'approved';
  workflow.approvedAt = new Date().toISOString();
  workflow.approvedBy = 'Dr Mohamed M.';
  // Approval itself is the release event. Colleagues cannot see a draft before this.
  workflow.releasedToAllAt = workflow.approvedAt;
  workflow.releasedToAllBy = 'Dr Mohamed M.';
  setRotaApprovalWorkflow(workflow);
  rotasticGenerationLockCache = {locked:true, unlock_at:rotasticNextWednesday2200After(workflow.approvedAt), week_start:workflow.snapshot?.weekStart || '', status:'approved'};
  rotasticRenderGenerationLock();

  isNextScheduleReleased = true;
  Storage.set('rotastic_next_schedule_released', 'true');
  setTimeout(function(){ rotasticBackendPersistRotaDocument('approved'); },0);

  // Dr Zaid receives his specific coordinator notification.
  requestBrowserNotificationPermission();
  sendTargetedPush(

    'ROTASTIC Rota Approved',
    'The Clini

function renderTickerCard(type, message, durationMs, fromHistory) {
  const immediate = type === 'sick' || type === 'greeting' || type === 'welcome';
  rotasticRunTickerTransition(function() {
    renderTickerCardNow(type, message, durationMs, fromHistory);
  }, immediate);
}

function choosePrayerMessage(prayerTimes) {
  const list = getRelevantPrayerMessages(prayerTimes);
  if (!list.length) return '';
  const message = list[prayerTickerIndex % list.length];
  prayerTickerIndex = (prayerTickerIndex + 1) % list.length;
  return message;
}



const ROTASTIC_GREETING_VIBES = [
  'Hope you have a smooth shift today.',
  'Wishing you a calm and safe shift.',
  'Hope today feels steady and manageable.',
  'Have a good shift and remember to hydrate.',
  'Wishing you a positive and uncomplicated day.',
  'Take it one patient at a time today.',
  'Hope the clinic stays calm and kind to you.',
  'Remember to pause, hydrate and reset when you can.',
  'Wishing you clear decisions and an easy workflow.',
  'Hope you get a few quiet moments between patients.',
  'Keep it calm, careful and compassionate today.',
  'Wishing you a safe shift and an easy handover.',
  'Look after yourself while you look after everyone else.',
  'Hope today brings more smiles than surprises.',
  'May your shift be smooth and your coffee stay warm.',
  'Wishing you a steady pace and a good team around you.',
  'Stay hydrated and give yourself a moment to breathe.',
  'Hope the day runs gently from start to finish.',
  'Wishing you good energy for the whole shift.',
  'Have a safe shift and be kind to yourself too.'
];

let rotasticLastGreetingVibe = '';

function nextGreetingVibe() {
  if (!ROTASTIC_GREETING_VIBES.length) return '';

  let options = ROTASTIC_GREETING_VIBES.filter(function(msg) {
    return msg !== rotasticLastGreetingVibe;
  });

  if (!options.length) options = ROTASTIC_GREETING_VIBES.slice();

  const message = options[Math.floor(Math.random() * options.length)];
  rotasticLastGreetingVibe = message;
  return message;
}

function buildRecurringDoctorGreeting() {
  const doctor = String(activeUserDocName || '').replace(/^Dr\.?\s*/i, '').trim();
  const first = doctor.split(/\s+/)[0] || 'Doctor';

  let hour = new Date().getHours();
  try {
    hour = parseInt(
      new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-GB', {
        timeZone:'Asia/Qatar',
        hour:'2-digit',
        hour12:false
      }).format(new Date()),
      10
    );
  } catch (e) {}

  const greeting =
    hour < 12 ? 'Good morning' :
    hour < 17 ? 'Good afternoon' :
    'Good evening';

  const wish = nextGreetingVibe();
  return greeting + ', Dr ' + first + '.\n' + wish;
}

async function renderNextTickerSlot() {
  const ticker = document.getElementById('swapsNewsTicker');
  if (!ticker) return;

  if (rotasticActiveTab !== 'schedule') {
    ticker.classList.add('hidden');
    return;
  }

  if (!profileSetupIsComplete()) {
    renderPersistentFirstSetupTicker();
    return;
  }

  const launchNotification = rotasticTakeLaunchTickerNotification();
  if (launchNotification) {
    rotasticMarkNotificationShownOnce(launchNotification);
    const launchText = '🔔 ' + String(launchNotification.message || 'You have a new ROTASTIC notification.');
    renderTickerCard('notification', launchText, 15000);
    return;
  }

  // After all launch notifications have appeared once, preserve the standard
  // greeting → centre identity → normal announcement sequence.
  if (!rotasticOpeningSequenceHasRun && !rotasticOpeningTickerActive) {
    startOpeningTickerSequence();
    return;

  ]);

    ? tickerFetches[0].value
    : null;

  tickerSlotCounter += 1;

  const meta = getActiveTickerMeta();

  // Live rota changes are highlighted in the same top ticker with a distinct style.

      renderTickerCard('rota', rotaAlert.message, 8500);
      return;

    );
  } catch (e) {}

  if (hour >= 17) return 'fa-solid fa-moon text-indigo-300 rotastic-moon-animate';
  if (hour >= 12) return 'fa-solid fa-cloud-sun text-amber-300 rotastic-cloudsun-animate';

}

function setTickerBadge(type, message) {
  const badge = document.getElementById('tickerTypeBadge');
  const icon = document.getElementById('tickerTypeIcon');
  const label = document.getElementById('tickerTypeText');
  const sourceLogo = document.getElementById('tickerSourceLogo');
  const fallbackTitle = document.getElementById('tickerFallbackTitle');
  if (!badge || !icon || !label || !fallbackTitle) return;

  badge.className = 'ticker-type-badge flex-shrink-0';

  const config = {
    prayer:  {cls:'ticker-prayer', icon:'fa-solid fa-mosque'},
    clinical:{cls:'ticker-clinical', icon:'fa-solid fa-stethoscope'},
    sick:    {cls:'ticker-sick', icon:'fa-solid fa-triangle-exclamation'},
    admin:   {cls:'ticker-admin', icon:'fa-solid fa-bullhorn'},
    qatar:   {cls:'ticker-qatar', icon:'fa-solid fa-trophy'},
    qnanews: {cls:'ticker-qnanews', icon:'fa-solid fa-newspaper'},
    traffic: {cls:'ticker-traffic', icon:'fa-solid fa-car-side'},
    rota:    {cls:'ticker-rota-change', icon:'fa-solid fa-arrows-rotate'},
    welcome: {cls:'ticker-welcome', icon:getGreetingTickerIcon()},
    greeting:{cls:'ticker-welcome', icon:getGreetingTickerIcon()},
    stat:    {cls:'ticker-stat', icon:'fa-solid fa-chart-column'},
    medicalnews:{cls:'ticker-medical-news', icon:'fa-solid fa-notes-medical'},
    worldnews:{cls:'ticker-world-news', icon:'fa-solid fa-earth-americas'},
    alarabiyanews:{cls:'ticker-world-news', icon:'fa-solid fa-newspaper'},
    reutersnews:{cls:'ticker-world-news', icon:'fa-solid fa-newspaper'},
    nhsinfo:{cls:'ticker-nhs-info', icon:'fa-solid fa-briefcase-medical'},
    safepractice:{cls:'ticker-clinical', icon:'fa-solid fa-shield-heart'},
    encouragement:{cls:'ticker-welcome', icon:'fa-solid fa-heart'},
    centre:  {cls:'', icon:'fa-solid fa-hospital'},
    general: {cls:'', icon:'fa-solid fa-wave-square'}
  }[type] || {cls:'', icon:'fa-solid fa-wave-square'};

  if (config.cls) badge.classList.add(config.cls);

  const displayTitle = getTickerDisplayTitle(type);
  const source = getTickerSourceMeta(type, message);
  const isGreeting = type === 'welcome' || type === 'greeting';

  icon.className = config.icon;
  icon.classList.remove('hidden');
  fallbackTitle.classList.add('hidden');
  label.textContent = '';
  label.title = '';

  if (sourceLogo) {
    sourceLogo.onerror = function() {
      sourceLogo.classList.add('hidden');
      sourceLogo.removeAttribute('src');
      badge.classList.remove('has-source-logo');

      if (isGreeting) {
        icon.classList.remove('hidden');
        fallbackTitle.classList.add('hidden');
        label.textContent = '';
      } else {
        icon.classList.add('hidden');
        fallbackTitle.innerHTML = displayTitle.replace(/\n/g, '<br>');
        fallbackTitle.classList.remove('hidden');
        label.textContent = '';
      }
    };

    if (source && !isGreeting) {
      sourceLogo.src =
        'https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=' +
        encodeURIComponent(source.domain) +
        '&sz=64';
      sourceLogo.alt = source.name + ' logo';
      sourceLogo.title = 'Source: ' + source.name;
      sourceLogo.classList.remove('hidden');
      badge.classList.add('has-source-logo');
      icon.classList.add('hidden');
      fallbackTitle.classList.add('hidden');

      const sourceName = compactTickerSourceName(source.name);
      label.textContent = sourceName;
      label.title = source.name;
      badge.title = 'Source: ' + source.name;
      return;
    }

    sourceLogo.classList.add('hidden');
    sourceLogo.removeAttribute('src');
    sourceLogo.alt = '';
    sourceLogo.title = '';
    badge.classList.remove('has-source-logo');
    badge.removeAttribute('title');
  }

  if (isGreeting) {
    icon.className = getGreetingTickerIcon();
    icon.classList.remove('hidden');
    fallbackTitle.classList.add('hidden');
    label.textContent = '';
  } else {
    icon.classList.add('hidden');
    fallbackTitle.innerHTML = displayTitle.replace(/\n/g, '<br>');
    fallbackTitle.classList.remove('hidden');
    label.textContent = '';
  }
}

function setTickerFullWidthMode(enabled) {
  const row = document.getElementById('tickerInnerRow');
  if (!row) return;
  row.classList.toggle('ticker-fullwidth-mode', !!enabled);
}


let rotasticSickInterruptTimer = null;
let rotasticSickTakeoverActive = false;


function formatSickBreakingMessage(rawItem, meta) {
  const raw = String(rawItem || '').trim();

  let doctorName =
    String(meta?.doctor || meta?.physician || meta?.name || '').trim();

  if (!doctorName) {
    const match =
      raw.match(/Dr\.?\s+[A-Za-z][A-Za-z .'-]*/i) ||
      raw.match(/(?:sick|reported sick|sick leave)[:\s-]+([A-Za-z][A-Za-z .'-]+)/i);

    if (match) {
      doctorName = String(match[0] || match[1] || '').trim();
    }
  }

  if (doctorName && !/^Dr\.?/i.test(doctorName)) {
    doctorName = 'Dr ' + doctorName;
  }

  if (!doctorName) {
    doctorName = 'A colleague';
  }

  return doctorName + ' reported sick today.\nGet well soon 🤒💐';
}


function isWithinFirstHourOfSickDoctorsShift(meta, rawItem) {
  let doctor =
    String(meta?.doctor || meta?.physician || meta?.name || '').trim();

  if (!doctor) {
    const match = String(rawItem || '').match(/Dr\.?\s+[A-Za-z][A-Za-z .'-]*/i);
    if (match) doctor = String(match[0] || '').trim();
  }

  // If doctor cannot be resolved, retain the existing priority window rather
  // than suppressing a clinically important sick alert.
  if (!doctor) return true;

  const day = getTodayScheduleDay();
  if (!day) return true;

  const timing = getDoctorShiftTiming(day, doctor);
  if (!timing) return true;

  let startMinutes = null;

  if (timing.label && timing.label.indexOf('09:00–16:00') !== -1) {
    startMinutes = 9 * 60;
  } else if (timing.label && timing.label.indexOf('14:00–20:00') !== -1) {
    startMinutes = 14 * 60;
  } else if (timing.period === 'AM') {
    startMinutes = 7 * 60;
  } else if (timing.period === 'PM') {
    startMinutes = 16 * 60;
  }

  if (startMinutes === null) return true;

  const parts = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-GB', {
    timeZone:'Asia/Qatar',
    hour:'2-digit',
    minute:'2-digit',
    hour12:false
  }).formatToParts(new Date());

  const hour = Number(parts.find(function(p){ return p.type === 'hour'; })?.value || 0);
  const minute = Number(parts.find(function(p){ return p.type === 'minute'; })?.value || 0);
  const nowMinutes = hour * 60 + minute;

  return nowMinutes >= startMinutes && nowMinutes < (startMinutes + 60
=== ticker-prayer @ 1678409 ===
'sick') {
    const badge = document.createElement('span');
    badge.className = 'ticker-breaking-chip';
    badge.textContent = 'BREAKING';

    const body = document.createElement('span');
    body.className = 'ticker-breaking-body';

    const sickLines = String(message || '').split('\n').filter(Boolean);
    if (sickLines.length > 1) {
      sickLines.forEach(function(line, index) {
        const lineEl = document.createElement('span');
        lineEl.className = 'ticker-breaking-line';
        lineEl.textContent = line;
        body.appendChild(lineEl);
      });
    } else {
      body.textContent = message;
    }

    card.appendChild(badge);
    card.appendChild(body);
  } else if (type === 'prayerMoment') {
    const badge = document.createElement('span');
    badge.className = 'ticker-prayer-moment-chip';

    const logo = document.createElement('img');
    logo.className = 'ticker-prayer-moment-logo';
    logo.src = 'https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=aladhan.com&sz=64';
    logo.alt = 'Prayer time source';
    logo.referrerPolicy = 'no-referrer';

    const label = document.createElement('span');
    label.textContent = 'PRAYER TIME';

    badge.appendChild(logo);
    badge.appendChild(label);

    const body = document.createElement('span');
    body.className = 'ticker-prayer-moment-body';
    body.textContent = message;

    card.appendChild(badge);
    card.appendChild(body);
  } else {
    card.textContent = message;
  }

  content.appendChild(card);

  if (headlineTickerTimer) clearTimeout(headlineTickerTimer);
  headlineTickerTimer = setTimeout(function() {
    if (type === 'sick') {
      rotasticSickTakeoverActive = false;
    }

    // Keep the takeover visible until the next message renderer replaces it.
    renderNextTickerSlot();
  }, type === 'sick'
      ? 5000
      : type === 'prayerMoment'
        ? (durationMs || 6500)
        : (durationMs || getTickerDisplayDuration(message, 7000)));
}

function renderCentreIdentityCard() {
  if (rotasticActiveTab !== 'schedule') return;

  // Preserve the original majestic slow zoom / letter-spacing animation.
  rotasticRunTickerTransition(function() {
    renderFullWidthTickerTakeover(
      'centre',
      'Abu Nakhla Health Centre',
      10000
    );
    scheduleSickTakeoverInterrupt();
  }, false);
}

const rotasticTickerHistory = [];
let rotasticTickerHistoryCursor = -1;
let rotasticTickerHistoryBrowsing = false;
let rotasticTickerTouchStartX = 0;
let rotasticTickerTouchStartY = 0;

function rememberRotasticTickerItem(type, message) {
  if (rotasticTickerHistoryBrowsing) return;
  const text = String(message || '').trim();
  if (!text) return;

  const last = rotasticTickerHistory[rotasticTickerHistory.length - 1];
  if (last && last.type === type && last.message === text) {
    rotasticTickerHistoryCursor = rotasticTickerHistory.length - 1;
    return;
  }

  rotasticTickerHistory.push({
    type:type || 'general',
    message:text,
    medicalLink:type === 'medicalnews' ? rotasticCurrentMedicalLink : ''
  });

  if (rotasticTickerHistory.length > 30) rotasticTickerHistory.shift();
  rotasticTickerHistoryCursor = rotasticTickerHistory.length - 1;
}

function showRotasticTickerHistory(index) {
  if (!rotasticTickerHistory.length) return;

  const bounded = Math.max(0, Math.min(index, rotasticTickerHistory.length - 1));
  const item = rotasticTickerHistory[bounded];
  if (!item) return;

  rotasticTickerHistoryCursor = bounded;
  rotasticTickerHistoryBrowsing = true;

  if (item.type === 'medicalnews') {
    rotasticCurrentMedicalLink = item.medicalLink || '';
  }

  renderTickerCard(item.type, item.message, 15000, true);
  rotasticTickerHistoryBrowsing = false;
}

function navigateRotasticTickerHistory(direction) {
  if (!rotasticTickerHistory.length) return;

  if (direction < 0) {
    if (rotasticTickerHistoryCursor <= 0) return;
    showRotasticTickerHistory(rotasticTickerHistoryCursor - 1);
    return;
  }

  if (rotasticTickerHistoryCursor < rotasticTickerHistory.length - 1) {
    showRotasticTickerHistory(rotasticTickerHistoryCursor + 1);
  } else {
    renderNextTickerSlot();
  }
}

function initializeTickerSwipeHistory() {
  const ticker = document.getElementById('swapsNewsTicker');
  if (!ticker || ticker.dataset.swipeHistoryBound === '1') return;
  ticker.dataset.swipeHistoryBound = '1';

  ticker.addEventListener('touchstart', function(event) {
    const touch = event.touches && event.touches[0];
    if (!touch) return;
    rotasticTickerTouchStartX = touch.clientX;
    rotasticTickerTouchStartY = touch.clientY;
  }, {passive:true});

  ticker.addEventListener('touchend', function(event) {
    const touch = event.changedTouches && event.changedTouches[0];
    if (!touch) return;

    const dx = touch.clientX - rotasticTickerTouchStartX;
    const dy = touch.clientY - rotasticTickerTouchStartY;

    // Horizontal swipe only; vertical scrolling/pull-to-refresh remains untouched.
    if (Math.abs(dx) < 48 || Math.abs(dx) <= Math.abs(dy) * 1.35) return;

    if (dx > 0) navigateRotasticTickerHistory(-1); // swipe right = previous
    else navigateRotasticTickerHistory(1);        // swipe left = next/resume
  }, {passive:true});
}


let rotasticTickerTransitionTimer = null;
let rotasticTickerTransitioning = false;

function rotasticRunTickerTransition(renderFn, immediate) {
  const row = document.getElementById('tickerInnerRow');
  const content = document.getElementById('tickerContent');
  if (!row || !content || immediate || !String(content.textContent || '').trim()) {
    renderFn();
    if (row) {
      row.classList.remove('rotastic-ticker-fade-out');
      row.classList.add('rotastic-ticker-fade-in');
      requestAnimationFrame(function() {
        requestAnimationFrame(function() { row.classList.remove('rotastic-ticker-fade-in'); });
      });
    }
    return;
  }

  if (rotasticTickerTransitionTimer) clearTimeout(rotasticTickerTransitionTimer);
  rotasticTickerTransitioning = true;
  row.classList.remove('rotastic-ticker-fade-in');
  row.classList.add('rotastic-ticker-fade-out');

  rotasticTickerTransitionTimer = setTimeout(function() {
    renderFn();
    row.classList.remove('rotastic-ticker-fade-out');
    row.classList.add('rotastic-ticker-fade-in');
    requestAnimationFrame(function() {
      requestAnimationFrame(function() {
        row.classList.remove('rotastic-ticker-fade-in');
        rotasticTickerTransitioning = false;
      });
    });
    rotasticTickerTransitionTimer = null;
  }, 320);
}

    const dy = touch.clientY - rotasticTickerTouchStartY;

    // Horizontal swipe only; vertical scrolling/pull-to-refresh remains untouched.
    if (Math.abs(dx) < 48 || Math.abs(dx) <= Math.abs(dy) * 1.35) return;

    if (dx > 0) navigateRotasticTickerHistory(-1); // swipe right = previous
    else navigateRotasticTickerHistory(1);        // swipe left = next/resume
  }, {passive:true});
}


let rotasticTickerTransitionTimer = null;
let rotasticTickerTransitioning = false;

function rotasticRunTickerTransition(renderFn, immediate) {
  const row = document.getElementById('tickerInnerRow');
  const content = document.getElementById('tickerContent');
  if (!row || !content || immediate || !String(content.textContent || '').trim()) {
    renderFn();
    if (row) {
      row.classList.remove('rotastic-ticker-fade-out');
      row.classList.add('rotastic-ticker-fade-in');
      requestAnimationFrame(function() {
        requestAnimationFrame(function() { row.classList.remove('rotastic-ticker-fade-in'); });
      });
    }
    return;
  }

  if (rotasticTickerTransitionTimer) clearTimeout(rotasticTickerTransitionTimer);
  rotasticTickerTransitioning = true;
  row.classList.remove('rotastic-ticker-fade-in');
  row.classList.add('rotastic-ticker-fade-out');

  rotasticTickerTransitionTimer = setTimeout(function() {
    renderFn();
    row.classList.remove('rotastic-ticker-fade-out');
    row.classList.add('rotastic-ticker-fade-in');
    requestAnimationFrame(function() {
      requestAnimationFrame(function() {
        row.classList.remove('rotastic-ticker-fade-in');
        rotasticTickerTransitioning = false;
      });
    });
    rotasticTickerTransitionTimer = null;
  }, 320);
}

function renderTickerCardNow(type, message, durationMs, fromHistory) {
  rotasticTickerLastRenderAt = Date.now();
  message = String(message || '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
  const knowledgeType = ['stat','medicalnews','worldnews','qnanews','alarabiyanews','reutersnews','nhsinfo'].includes(type);
  if (knowledgeType) message = fitNormalTickerCopy(message, 16, 112);
  else if (!['sick','centre','greeting','welcome','notification'].includes(type)) message = fitNormalTickerCopy(message, 18, 124) || message;
  if (!message) {
    message = nextDoctorEncouragement() || 'Wishing you a smooth and safe shift today.';
    type = 'encouragement';
  }
  if (rotasticActiveTab !== 'schedule') {
    const hiddenTicker = document.getElementById('swapsNewsTicker');
    if (hiddenT
=== ticker-prayer @ 1685852 ===
= document.getElementById('tickerContent');
  if (!ticker || !content) return;
  ticker.classList.remove('ticker-greeting-mode');
  setTickerFullWidthMode(false);
  ticker.classList.remove('hidden');
  content.innerHTML = '';
  setTickerBadge(type, message);
  if (!fromHistory) rememberRotasticTickerItem(type, message);
  const card = document.createElement('div');
  const leftAligned = ['safepractice','encouragement','stat','qatar','qnanews','medicalnews','worldnews','alarabiyanews','reutersnews','nhsinfo','prayer','admin','clinical','rota','general','notification'].includes(type);
  card.className = 'ticker-message-card ' + getTickerCopySizeClass(message) +
    (type === 'rota' ? ' ticker-rota-change-card' : '') +
    (leftAligned ? ' ticker-left-card' : '') +
    (type === 'prayer' ? ' ticker-prayer-card' : '');
  if (type === 'prayer') {
    const body = document.createElement('span');
    body.className = 'ticker-message-text';
    body.textContent = message;
    card.appendChild(body);
  } else if ((type === 'medicalnews' && rotasticCurrentMedicalLink) || (['qnanews','worldnews','alarabiyanews','reutersnews'].includes(type) && rotasticCurrentTickerLink)) {
    const link = document.createElement('a');
    link.className = 'ticker-medical-link';
    link.href = type === 'medicalnews' ? rotasticCurrentMedicalLink : rotasticCurrentTickerLink;
    link.target = '_blank';
    link.rel = 'noopener noreferrer';
    link.textContent = message;
    link.title = 'Open source';
    card.appendChild(link);
  } else {
    card.textContent = message;
  }
  content.appendChild(card);
  if (headlineTickerTimer) clearTimeout(headlineTickerTimer);
  const requestedDuration = Number(durationMs || 0);
  const comfortableDuration = getTickerDisplayDuration(message, 7800);
  const finalDuration = Math.max(requestedDuration, comfortableDuration);
  headlineTickerTimer = setTimeout(function() { renderNextTickerSlot(); }, finalDuration);
  scheduleSickTakeoverInterrupt();
}


function renderTickerCard(type, message, durationMs, fromHistory) {
  const immediate = type === 'sick' || type === 'greeting' || type === 'welcome';
  rotasticRunTickerTransition(function() {
    renderTickerCardNow(type, message, durationMs, fromHistory);
  }, immediate);
}

function choosePrayerMessage(prayerTimes) {
  const list = getRelevantPrayerMessages(prayerTimes);
  if (!list.length) return '';
  const message = list[prayerTickerIndex % list.length];
  prayerTickerIndex = (prayerTickerIndex + 1) % list.length;
  return message;
}



const ROTASTIC_GREETING_VIBES = [
  'Hope you have a smooth shift today.',
  'Wishing you a calm and safe shift.',
  'Hope today feels steady and manageable.',
  'Have a good shift and remember to hydrate.',
  'Wishing you a positive and uncomplicated day.',
  'Take it one patient at a time today.',
  'Hope the clinic stays calm and kind to you.',
  'Remember to pause, hydrate and reset when you can.',
  'Wishing you clear decisions and an easy workflow.',
  'Hope you get a few quiet moments between patients.',
  'Keep it calm, careful and compassionate today.',
  'Wishing you a safe shift and an easy handover.',
  'Look after yourself while you look after everyone else.',
  'Hope today brings more smiles than surprises.',
  'May your shift be smooth and your coffee stay warm.',
  'Wishing you a steady pace and a good team around you.',
  'Stay hydrated and give yourself a moment to breathe.',
  'Hope the day runs gently from start to finish.',
  'Wishing you good energy for the whole shift.',
  'Have a safe shift and be kind to yourself too.'
];

let rotasticLastGreetingVibe = '';

function nextGreetingVibe() {
  if (!ROTASTIC_GREETING_VIBES.length) return '';

  let options = ROTASTIC_GREETING_VIBES.filter(function(msg) {
    return msg !== rotasticLastGreetingVibe;
  });

  if (!options.length) options = ROTASTIC_GREETING_VIBES.slice();

  const message = options[Math.floor(Math.random() * options.length)];
  rotasticLastGreetingVibe = message;
  return message;
}

function buildRecurringDoctorGreeting() {
  const doctor = String(activeUserDocName || '').replace(/^Dr\.?\s*/i, '').trim();
  const first = doctor.split(/\s+/)[0] || 'Doctor';

  let hour = new Date().getHours();
  try {
    hour = parseInt(
      new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-GB', {
        timeZone:'Asia/Qatar',
        hour:'2-digit',
        hour12:false
      }).format(new Date()),
      10
    );
  } catch (e) {}

  const greeting =
    hour < 12 ? 'Good morning' :
    hour < 17 ? 'Good afternoon' :
    'Good evening';

  const wish = nextGreetingVibe();
  return greeting + ', Dr ' + first + '.\n' + wish;
}

async function renderNextTickerSlot() {
  const ticker = document.getElementById('swapsNewsTicker');
  if (!ticker) return;

  if (rotasticActiveTab !== 'schedule') {
    ticker.classList.add('hidden');
    return;
  }

  if (!profileSetupIsComplete()) {
    renderPersistentFirstSetupTicker();
    return;
  }

  const launchNotification = rotasticTakeLaunchTickerNotification();
  if (launchNotification) {
    rotasticMarkNotificationShownOnce(launchNotification);
    const launchText = '🔔 ' + String(launchNotification.message || 'You have a new ROTASTIC notification.');
    renderTickerCard('notification', launchText, 15000);
    return;
  }

  // After all launch notifications have appeared once, preserve the standard
  // greeting → centre identity → normal announcement sequence.
  if (!rotasticOpeningSequenceHasRun && !rotasticOpeningTickerActive) {
    startOpeningTickerSequence();
    return;
  }

  const tickerFetches = await Promise.allSettled([
    fetchAbuNakhlaPrayerTimes(),
    refreshLiveClinicalUpdates(),
    refreshQatarAchievementNews(),
    refreshLocalTrafficUpdate(),
    refreshExternalHeadlineFeeds()
  ]);
  const prayerTimes = tickerFetches[0] && tickerFetches[0].status === 'fulfilled'
    ? tickerFetches[0].value
    : null;

  tickerSlotCounter += 1;

  const rawItem = String(Storage.get('rotastic_breaking_news_item', '') || '').trim();
  const meta = getActiveTickerMeta();

  // Live rota changes are highlighted in the same top ticker with a distinct style.
  const rotaAlert = Storage.getJSON('rotastic_rota_change_alert', null);
  if (rotaAlert && rotaAlert.message && Number(rotaAlert.expiresAt || 0) > Date.now()) {
    if (tickerSlotCounter % 3 === 1) {
      renderTickerCard('rota', rotaAlert.message, 8500);
      return;
    }
  }

  // In the final 30 minutes, insert a concise, positive personal message plus
  // tomorrow's rota note without stopping the existing ticker/news rotation.
  const lastThirtyMessage = getLastThirtyMinuteTickerMessage();
  if (lastThirtyMessage && tickerSlotCounter % 3 === 1) {
    renderTickerCard('general', lastThirtyMessage, getTickerDisplayDuration(la
